### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 把文件路径直接跟在 `bun test` 后面就行：

```bash
bun test src/utils/parse.test.ts
```

也可以只写文件名的一部分，Bun 会匹配名字里包含它的测试文件，比如 `bun test parse`。如果还想只跑文件里的某个用例，加 `-t`：

```bash
bun test src/utils/parse.test.ts -t "空字符串"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to a database before the change is applied to the data files. If the system crashes midway, it replays that log on restart so committed work survives and half-finished work can be rolled back.
```

要做的：
- 按 review 里关于锁粒度的意见，把锁的范围缩小，让 compaction 期间不必锁住整个结构，只锁真正需要互斥的部分。

完成标准（请在报告里附上证据）：
- 改后的代码和具体改了哪些锁、锁的范围怎么变，写清楚。
- 相关测试和并发测试的运行输出；如果有新增的并发场景测试，也列出来。
- 说明这次改动是否影响 review 里提到的其他问题。

不在范围内：
- 不要推送到远端，不要更新或新开 PR，不要合并。推送和 PR 操作等用户点头再做。
```

}

}

load();

```
(earlier) user: Run hawdb's write benchmark every night and tell me how it went.
(earlier) partner ack: On it: every night at 23:00.  → task "Nightly hawdb write benchmark" working; its report: Night 3: 182k writes/s, p99 4.1 ms, level with last night (181k, 4.0 ms). Compaction stalls: 0.
(earlier) partner: Night 3: 182k writes/s at p99 4.1 ms, level with last night; no compaction stalls.
(earlier) Ducter offered on its own card: keep task "bench"'s rounds on a board?
--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] Done: the board is up with the nightly hawdb numbers, and it's filled with nights 2 and 3 so far.
  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb nightly benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1893 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest"><span class="k-none">Not yet</span></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="latestSub">Nightly hawdb write benchmark, runs at 23:00</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
function fmt(n){ return n >= 1000 ? Math.round(n / 1000) + 'k' : String(n); }
function row(parent, main, sub, value){
  var r = document.createElement('div');
  r.className = 'k-row';
  var m = document.createElement('div');
  m.className = 'k-row-main';
  m.textContent = main;
  var s = document.createElement('div');
  s.className = 'k-row-sub';
  s.textContent = sub;
  m.appendChild(s);
  var v = document.createElement('div');
  v.className = 'k-row-value';
  v.textContent = value;
  r.appendChild(m);
  r.appendChild(v);
  parent.appendChild(r);
}
function render(v){
  var nights = Array.isArray(v) ? v.slice() : [];
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  if (!nights.length) {
    var e = document.createElement('div');
    e.className = 'k-empty';
    e.textContent = 'No nights yet. The first report lands after the 23:00 run.';
    list.appendChild(e);
    return;
  }
  nights.sort(function(a, b){ return b.night - a.night; });
  var last = nights[0];
  document.getElementById('latest').textContent = fmt(last.writes) + ' writes/s';
  document.getElementById('latestSub').textContent = 'Night ' + last.night + ' · p99 ' + last.p99 + ' ms · compaction stalls ' + last.stalls;
  nights.forEach(function(n){
    row(list, 'Night ' + n.night, 'p99 ' + n.p99 + ' ms · stalls ' + n.stalls, fmt(n.writes) + ' writes/s');
  });
}
ducter.onChange(render);
ducter.get('nights').then ? ducter.get('nights').then(render) : render(ducter.get('nights'));
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"night":2,"writes":181000,"p99":4,"stalls":0},{"night":3,"writes":182000,"p99":4.1,"stalls":0}]}

function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const sum=xs.reduce((a,x)=>a+Number(x.yuan||0),0);
  document.getElementById("total").textContent=fmt(sum);
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length? xs.length+" 笔":"还没记";
  const ul=document.getElementById("list");
  ul.innerHTML="";
  document.getElementById("empty").hidden=xs.length>0;
  for(const x of xs){
    const row=document.createElement("div");
    row.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");
    main.className="k-row-main";
    main.textContent=x.what;
    const sub=document.createElement("div");
    sub.className="k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent=x.day;
    main.appendChild(sub);
    const val=document.createElement("div");
    val.className="k-row-value";
    val.textContent=fmt(x.yuan);
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(val);
    ul.appendChild(row);
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length? xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(sum) : "还没记");
}
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

  }
  persist(); resetForm(); drawAll();
};
$('cancel').onclick = () => { resetForm(); };
$('remove').onclick = () => {
  items = items.filter(i => i.id !== editId);
  persist(); resetForm(); drawAll();
};

function load(){

    if (typeof v === 'string') { try { v = JSON.parse(v); } catch (e) { v = []; } }
    items = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
    drawAll();
  });
}

ducter.onChange(() => load());
drawForm();
load();

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: a page with your four PRs, and you tick each one off as it lands. The count at the top drops as you go. It's on your shelf under PRs waiting on someone.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on someone", with Open] the app's file now (v2, 2478 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <section class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="left">–</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">tick each one as it lands</div>
  </section>

  <h2 class="k-h">Your PRs</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
</main>

<style>
  .k-row { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: center; gap: 4px 10px; }
  .k-row input[type="checkbox"] { width: 18px; height: 18px; accent-color: var(--d-accent); flex: none; }
  .k-row-main { flex: 1; min-width: 0; color: var(--d-ink); }
  .k-row-sub { width: 100%; padding-left: 28px; color: var(--d-muted); font-size: 13px; }
  .k-row.landed .k-row-main { text-decoration: line-through; color: var(--d-muted); }
  .k-row.landed .k-row-sub::after { content: " · landed"; color: var(--d-good); }
</style>

<script>
  var items = [];
  var listEl = document.getElementById('list');

  function landedMap() {
    var m = {};
    items.forEach(function (it) { if (it.landed) m[it.id] = true; });
    return m;
  }

  function paint() {
    listEl.innerHTML = '';
    var left = 0;
    items.forEach(function (it) {
      if (!it.landed) left++;
      var row = document.createElement('label');
      row.className = 'k-row' + (it.landed ? ' landed' : '');
      var box = document.createElement('input');
      box.type = 'checkbox';
      box.checked = !!it.landed;
      box.addEventListener('change', function () {
        it.landed = box.checked;
        ducter.set('landed', landedMap());
        paint();
      });
      var main = document.createElement('span');
      main.className = 'k-row-main';
      main.textContent = it.title;
      var sub = document.createElement('span');
      sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
      sub.textContent = it.note;
      row.appendChild(box);
      row.appendChild(main);
      row.appendChild(sub);
      listEl.appendChild(row);
    });
    document.getElementById('left').textContent = String(left);
    document.getElementById('sub').textContent = 'of ' + items.length + ' PRs · tick each one as it lands';
    ducter.summary(left + ' of ' + items.length + ' PRs still waiting');
  }

  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('items')).then(function (saved) {
    items = saved || [];
    return Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed'));
  }).then(function (landed) {
    landed = landed || {};
    items.forEach(function (it) { it.landed = !!landed[it.id]; });
    paint();
  });
</script>
; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"ducter-412","title":"wey-gu/ducter#412 · compaction cut","note":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday"},{"id":"mem-10287","title":"nowledge-co/mem#10287 · retry on 502","note":"On CI, failing"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","title":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 · flaky compaction test","note":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter-418","title":"wey-gu/ducter#418 · apps bar","note":"Draft"}]}

```
